# Session Status — index

> Index, not log. Per-session handoffs in [`docs/session-state/`](./docs/session-state/) — this file points at them.
> Briefing API parses `## TODO` and `## Blockers` (keep those headings populated).
> Older sessions (pre-2026-05-24) live in [`docs/session-state/archive-pre-2026-05-24.md`](./docs/session-state/archive-pre-2026-05-24.md) plus the dated `.html` files alongside.

## Cold-start protocol

1. **Issue-driven**: `KUBEDOJO_ISSUE=N bash scripts/cold-start.sh` after reading the issue verbatim.
   **Standalone**: `bash scripts/cold-start.sh` (add `--manifest` for route discovery).
   The script does services-up, `git status`, pending decisions, briefing, orient, handoff pointer. Exit 0 with `STATUS.md` fallback on API failure.
2. Scan [`docs/decisions/pending/`](./docs/decisions/pending/) before unrelated work (also surfaced by the script). **Approved decisions live in [`docs/decisions/`](./docs/decisions/)** — `2026-05-29-1639-ai-engineering-consolidation.md` is now fully executed (§1–4, session 70); nothing pending awaiting execution.
3. Read **Latest handoff** below only if briefing/orient leave a narrative gap.

## Active policies

- **Tiered handoff thresholds + brain-rot canary (s119; re-grounded s150)**: handoff target **re-grounded 2026-06-15 to ~820K** (`~/.claude/context-check.sh` tiers `500/600/700`→**`650/750/820K`**). Rationale (verified vs the authoritative `claude-api` pricing ref): Opus 4.8 has **NO long-context premium** at 1M (standard pricing all the way up) → no price cliff; combined with the canary's **no-measured-rot** finding, the binding constraints are the **1M HARD WALL + handoff continuity-risk, NOT cost** → **hand off LATE** (~820K = 1M − ~150-200K margin), not at the old conservative 700K. The warm per-turn tax is tiny (ctx×$0.50/1M ≈ $0.35/turn at 700K); the **real cost is the cache-cold re-entry** (ctx×$6.25/1M ≈ $4-5) when an idle gap exceeds the 5-min cache TTL → **batch work to avoid cold re-entries** rather than hand off early. 750K tier = run a context-integrity self-check (**`scripts/quality/context_canary.py`**: `mint` immutable anchors early; answer FROM MEMORY each checkpoint; SCRIPT diffs vs frozen truth ≥0.75/anchor, ≥0.85 pass → drift = hand off NOW regardless of tokens). Always MEASURE tokens from the transcript (self-estimate ~1.8× high). **Validated s119: 8 checkpoints 542K→654K all 10/10 — opus-4.8 NO measured rot at 65% of 1M.** Log: `logs/canary/canary_log.csv`. (`context-check.sh` + `statusline.sh` both updated to 650/750/820 + /1M display; autocompact already at 1M in `start-claude.sh`.)

- **Reviewer rotation expanded (validated 2026-06-04, session 102 candidate trial)**: **grok-build-0.1** (grok-4.3 via `hermes --provider xai-oauth -m grok-build-0.1`) and **agy** with `--model "Claude Sonnet 4.6 (Thinking)" / "Claude Opus 4.6 (Thinking)" / gemini-3.1-pro-high` are now first-class cross-family REVIEW lanes alongside opus/cursor — spreads load off opus before the June-15 programmatic-pool billing change. **grok-build = EXCELLENT** (caught real fact/CLI errors opus missed/false-confirmed; complementary, not a replacement — opus still owns deep lab-runnability). **agy·Claude/Gemini-3.1-Pro = STRONG.** **DROP for review: grok-4.20-reasoning** (over-approves, missed a P1) and **agy·GPT-OSS-120B** (hallucinates whole findings, would introduce errors). **The model under `agy` matters — pick it explicitly; agy mislabels its own model in output.** **ALL candidate "Verified/ran" provenance is fabricated → ground-check the FINDINGS (web-verify), never the claimed verification.** Reviewers are still REVIEW-only (no write/edit for agy/grok). - **Claude/opus REVIEW seat = in-session, never `claude -p` (locked 2026-06-12, session 135, user EMPHATIC)**: do NOT dispatch `--agent claude` / `claude -p` / Agent-SDK / GH-Actions-Claude — it draws the separate capped programmatic pool (not the main quota) AND is currently broken (`claude-code@2.1.175` native-binary postinstall fails). The Claude reviewer is the **interactive orchestrator, INLINE** (main quota): do it **EARLY** while context is light; if context is heavy, **DEFER to the next interactive session's start** (top-of-handoff `Claude review PENDING: <module/PR>`; that module's merge waits a session) — do **NOT** spawn a subagent (its ~2–3M project reload is ~50–150× the tokens of inline/deferred for the identical verdict, duplicating a session boot you pay anyway) and do NOT cram a depleted session. "Next session" = the next MANUAL interactive session (cold-start reads the handoff), NOT cron / `/schedule` / `-p`. Other families still dispatch normally. **Supersedes the "minimize-`-p` / use-freely-this-week" framing in the bullet below.** Memories: `feedback_claude_billing_reroute`, `feedback_opus_subagent_review_economics`.

## End-of-session ritual

1. Write today's full handoff to `docs/session-state/YYYY-MM-DD-<topic>.html`.
2. Add a row at top of **Latest handoff** (1-line summary + link). Keep ≤3 rows here; shift older rows to the `docs/session-state/` pointer.
3. Update **TODO** + **Blockers** (briefing API depends on these headings).
4. Commit with `docs(status): handoff <date> — <topic>` style.
